# sdf

# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
= E1 * R2/(R1+R2)
= 15 * 100/(100+100)
= 7.5 V
 ^lt7SXr4I

= E2 * R1/(R1+R2)
= 5 * 100/(100+100)
= 2.5 V
 ^AVTPCSTf

UAB0 = UAB01 + UAB02
= E1 * R2/(R1+R2) + E2 * R1/(R1+R2)
= 10 V ^msaTt97C

Rth = R1*R2/(R1+R2) = 50hm
  ^nvlRGffr

On débranche la résistance R puis on fait le théorème de superposition ^Ij2BKBdV

UAB = UR = Eth*R/(Rth+R)
= 6.7V
 ^cvNoZgtd

Ir = UR/R 
= 6.7/100
= 67 mA ^CtWTbiZL
